Finite diff:
```
def gradient_log(a, h=1e-5):
    n=len(a); grad=np.zeros(n); base=obj(a)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    for i in range(n):
       zp=z.copy(); zm=z.copy()
       zp[i]+=h; zm[i]-=h
       ap=np.exp(zp); ap/=ap.sum(); am=np.exp(zm); am/=am.sum()
       fp=obj(ap); fm=obj(am)
       grad[i]=(fp-fm)/(2*h)
    # Since a normalized, gradient constrained to simplex. Remove mean to remove scale: grad -= grad.mean()/n? Actually radial direction: log a all +constant leads same normalized a, objective unchanged. The derivative along constant is zero theoretically; numerical noise. Project grad onto sum(grad)=0: grad -= grad.mean().
```

**Local optimizer using numerical gradient**:
```
def normalize(a):
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s==0: raise
    return a/s

def score(a):
    # use exact evaluator on list; assume normalized sum=1
    try: val=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a])
    except: val=inf
    return val if finite else inf
```
But evaluate_sequence internally clips and normalizes? no. Use normalized to avoid cap. If a has zeros okay.

def run_lbfgs(z0, beta, time_limit):
    n_var = n-1
    def fun_grad(z):
        z = np.asarray(z)
        if time exceeded: raise Timeout
        Z = sum(exp(z)); a = concat(exp(z), [1/Z])
        c = np.convolve(a,a)
        M = c.max(); zc = beta*(c-M)
        w = exp(zc); wsum = sum(w)
        if wsum ==0 or not finite: ...
        w /= wsum
        F = (M + log(wsum)/beta)?? Since LSE=log(sum exp(beta(c-M))) + M = M + log(wsum)/beta. Yes.
        g = active_grad(w,a)
        gz = a[:-1]*(g[:-1] - np.dot(g,a))
        return F, gz
    res = minimize(fun_grad, z0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':..., 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8})
```

**LP matrix building**:
For speed, build dense A with indexing:
```
n=len(a); m=2*n-1
k = np.arange(m)[:,None]
i = np.arange(n)[None,:]
j = k - i
mask = (j>=0)&(j<n)
A = np.where(mask, a[np.clip(j,0,n-1)], 0.0)
```
This allocates 381x191, cheap. Could precompute mask and indices once for n. At each call set A = a[idx[mask]]? Precompute for n=191: list of (row, col, value_index). Since A[k,i]=a[j], j=k-i. We can precompute `rows`, `cols`, `val_idx`. Then `A = np.zeros((m,n)); A[rows,cols]=a[val_idx]`. Sparse easier.
Use `scipy.sparse.csr_matrix((a[val_idx], (rows, cols)), shape=(m,n))`. LP with sparse reduces memory. Precompute once per n.

LP call:
```
def solve_lp(a, M, timeout=3.0):
    n=a.size
    if n not in precompute...; build
    A = csr_matrix((np.asarray(a)[val_idx], (rows, cols)), shape=(m,n))
    c = -np.ones(n)
    b_ub = np.full(m, M)
    res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'time_limit': timeout, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9})
```
If timeout, res.status maybe 2? Check.

def lp_optimize(a0, stop_time, max_iters=200, lp_timeout=2.0):
   a = norm(a0); best = exact; best_a=a
   for it in range(max_iters):
     if time>stop_time-5 break
     c=conv(a); M=max(c)
     if M < best_M...
     # solve with current a (positivized) and M*(1+1e-9)
     g = solve_lp(a, M*(1+1e-8), timeout=lp_timeout)
     if g is None: break
     ssum=g.sum(); if ssum<=0 break
     gnorm=g/ssum
     # line search t in [1,0.8,...]
     improved=False
     for t in ...:
        cand=(1-t)*a+t*gnorm; if min<0 clip; normalize
        sc=score_fast/cand; if sc<best_score: accept update a,best
     if no improve: if max_iters... maybe relax M by 1+1e-4 or break after many
```

- Fβ and gradient:
```
def fun_grad(z):
   if timeout: raise
   a = softmax_n1(z)
   c = np.convolve(a,a)
   M = c.max()
   # stable weights
   zc = beta*(c - M)
   # zc <=0. If beta very high, underflow okay.
   e = np.exp(zc)
   s = e.sum()
   if s==0 or not finite: e[np.argmax(c)]=1; s=1
   w = e/s
   F = M + np.log(s)/beta
   g = zero
   tol = 1e-12
   act = np.where(w > tol)[0]
   for k in act:
      wk=w[k]
      lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
      # a[k-hi : k-lo+1]
      g[lo:hi+1] += 2*wk*a[k-hi:k-lo+1]
   ga = float(np.dot(g,a))
   gz = a[:-1]*(g[:-1]-ga)
   # Maybe scale gradient to avoid overflow: if max abs huge, clip?
   return F, gz
```
Check gradient of F = M + log(s)/β (where s=∑ exp(β(c-M))). Derivative = (1/β)∑e*β∂c - M∂s/s = ∑w ∂c - M∂M + M∂M = ∑w∂c. Our g = ∑w∂c. Good.

def _get_parent():
    try:
       h = globals().get('height_sequence_1')
       if h is not None:
           p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
           p = np.clip(p,0,1000)
           if p.size>0 and np.isfinite(p).all() and p.sum()>0:
               return p
    except: pass
    return np.ones(191)

**score function**:
```
def _score_self(a, exact=False):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return inf
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 0.01: # exact would inf but if normalized sum1 no
        return inf
    # For fast exact formula under shown:
    c = np.convolve(a,a)
    mb = float(c.max())
    if not (np.isfinite(mb) and mb>=0): return inf
    return 2.0*a.size*mb/(s*s)
```

_lp_cache = {}
def _get_lp_matrix_indices(n):
    if n not in cache:
       m=2*n-1
       rows=[]; cols=[]; vals=[]
       for k in range(m):
          for i in range(n):

**Softmax n1 implementation**:

def _asoftmax_n1(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.clip(z, -50, 50)
    e = np.exp(z)
    Z = e.sum() + 1.0
    a = np.empty(N)
    a[:-1] = e / Z
    a[-1] = 1.0 / Z
    return a
```

**Fβ with pivot**:
```
def _make_lbfgs_fun(a0, beta, stop_time):
   p = int(np.argmax(a0))
   if a0[p] <= 1e-12: p=0; a0 = a0 + eps
   z0 = np.log(np.concatenate(([a0[i]/a0[p] for i in range(p)], [a0[i]/a0[p] for i in range(p+1)])))
   def fun_grad(z):
       if time>stop_time: raise Timeout
       a = _asoft_a_n1(z,p)
       c=conv(a)
       M=c.max()
       # weights
       # ensure c finite
       zc = beta*(c-M); zc=np.clip(zc,-700,0)
       e=np.exp(zc); s=e.sum(); if s==0 or not finite: e[argmax(c)]=1; s=1
       w=e/s
       F=M + np.log(s)/beta
       g = _grad_active(w,a)
       ga=np.dot(g,a)
       gz = np.empty(n-1)
       # for all i != p:
       mask = np.arange(n)!=p
       gz = a[mask]*(g[mask]-ga)
       # optionally scale/clip
       return F, gz
   return z0, fun_grad
```
But if p=0, `z` order all except 0. Mapping back:

idx = [i for i in range(n) if i != p]
a[idx] = e/den
a[p]=1/den
```

**Gradient active vectorized**:
```
def _grad_active(w, a):
    n=a.size; g=np.zeros(n); m=w.size
    # threshold relative to w.sum? w sums 1; use 1e-12
    for k in range(m):
       wk = w[k]
       if wk <= 1e-12: continue
       lo = k - (n-1)
       if lo < 0: lo=0
       hi = k
       if hi > n-1: hi=n-1
       # slice length hi-lo+1
       g[lo:hi+1] += 2.0*wk*a[k-hi:k-lo+1]
    return g
```
Let's test small n=3: a=[a0,a1,a2]. conv c: c0=a0^2; c1=a0a1+a1a0=2a0a1; c2=2a0a2+a1^2; c3=2a1a2; c4=a2^2. For k=1, lo=0 hi=1, a[k-hi:k-lo+1]=a[0:2]=[a0,a1]; g[0:2]+=2wk*[a0,a1]. Gradient wrt a0 of c1=2a1? Wait derivative c1=2a0a1, grad a0=2a1, but formula gives 2a0. Hmm error. Because convolution c_k = ∑_{i+j=k} a_i a_j. For k=1, c1=a0a1 + a1a0=2a0a1. Derivative wrt a0: 2a1, not 2a0. My earlier formula g_j = 2 a_{k-j} is correct: for j=0, a_{1}=a1. In slice mapping I had a[k-hi] for j=lo? Need reverse. Let's derive: j from lo to hi. derivative for j = 2*a_{k-j}. Sequence for j=lo..hi corresponds indices [k-hi, k-hi+1, ..., k-lo]. So array should be `a[k-hi : k-lo+1]` as I wrote, but for k=1, hi=1, lo=0 => a[0:2]=[a0,a1]; expected [a1,a0]. So need **reverse**: `a[k-lo : k-hi-1 : -1]` or `a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]`. For j=lo, k-hi; derivative a[k-lo]? Wait j=lo -> k-j = k-lo, so first value should a[k-lo]. j=hi -> a[k-hi]. So reversed slice. Use:
```
seg = a[k-lo:k-hi-1:-1]  # from k-lo down to k-hi
```
Check k=1, lo0 hi1 -> a[1:-2:-1]=a[1], but length? Need include hi: stop = k-hi-1 = -1? `a[1:-1:-1]` gives a1? **LP optimize from multiple inits**:
```
def _lp_search(a0, stop_time, max_lp=50):
    a=norm(a0); best=score(a); best_a=a
    cycles=0
    while time<stop_time and cycles<max_lp:
       if best no improve 15: maybe relax; if 30 break
       c=conv(a); M=c.max()
       # if fast score < best: update
       g=solve_lp(a, M*(1+1e-9), timeout=2.0)
       if g None: break
       gn=g/g.sum()
       improved=False
       for t in ...:
          cand=...; sc=score(cand)
          if sc < best*(1-1e-11): update a,best; improved=True; cycles=0; break
       if not improved:
          # try M relaxed
          g2=solve_lp(a, M*(1+1e-5), timeout=1.0)
          ... if improve
          cycles+=1
    return best_a
```

**L-BFGS smooth optimizer**:
```
def _lbfgs_search(a0, stop_time, betas=(50,200,1000,3000), maxiter=60):
   a=norm(a0); best=score(a); best_a=a
   for beta in betas:
      if time>stop_time-30: break
      z0, fun, p = _make_fun(a,beta, stop_time-max(20,0.05*remaining))
      # We want allow each beta maybe time left/len
      try res=minimize(... options maxiter=maxiter, ftol=1e-12, gtol=1e-8, maxls=20, disp=False)
      except Exception: res=None
      if res and res.x finite:
          cand=softmax_n1(res.x,p); normalize; sc=score(cand); if sc<best: update; a=cand
      else: # if timeout exception, no
      # optionally use current a for next beta
   return best_a
```

def _smooth(a, sigma):
    kernel = np.exp(-0.5*(np.arange(-w,w+1)/sigma)**2)
    c = np.convolve(a, kernel, mode='same')
    c = np.maximum(c,0)
    return c/c.sum()

**Implement `_normalize`**:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       return np.ones(a.size if a.size>0 else 1)/... 
    return a/s
```
If all zeros, uniform length same.

**Resample**:
```
def _resample(a,L):
   if L==a.size: return a.copy()
   x0=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); x1=np.linspace(0,1,L)
   b=np.interp(x1,x0,a)
   return _normalize(b)
```

def _fd_polish(a0, stop_time, max_iters=100, h=1e-3):

   for it:
     if time break
     grad = finite diff of score(softmax_n1(z,p)) (central if possible)
     # If grad all close zero break
     scale=max(abs(grad)); if <1e-10: break
     alpha = 0.1/scale; for mult try negative gradient, accept if score improves.
     if no, random.

```

**Now let's refine LP solve**: Danning. We can precompute indices for N as local in `_lp_optimize`? Use global cache by N. Function:
```
_lp_cache = {}
def _get_conv_matrix_indices(n):
   if n in _lp_cache: return ...
   rows=[]; cols=[]; vals=[]
   for k in range(2*n-1):
      lo = max(0, k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
      # For row k, cols i from lo to hi; val j=k-i
      rows.extend(k for _ in range(lo,hi+1))
      cols.extend(range(lo,hi+1))
      vals.extend(range(k-hi, k-lo+1))  # k-i for i=lo..hi => descending
```
Wait if cols i=lo..hi, val index j=k-i: for i=lo, j=k-lo; for i=hi,j=k-hi. So values descending. Good. Total n^2.
```
   rows=np.array(rows); cols=np.array(cols); vals=np.array(vals)
   _lp_cache[n]=(rows,cols,vals,2*n-1)
```

Could instead solve LP at a target M_new = desired lower; but nonlinear. The max sum piecewise linear in M: S(M). We want largest M_new such that S(M_new)≥1. That is the minimal cross bound to have a probability. If M_new < current M, found g with cross bound M_new. This could be stronger. How to find? Bisection on M between average lower 1/(2n-1) and current M; but LP expensive. However if we can solve one LP at current M, S(M) known. The function S(M) linear between breakpoints. Not enough. Could use dual: minimal max cross for some g? Not.

Maybe after LP at current M, use its dual optimal y to form a subgradient active set? Too much.

**Alternative: Solve LP to find g that minimizes ||g*g||∞ given cross? Hard.

**Iterative regularization**: We can maintain a bag of candidate distributions (LP outputs) and take convex hull / blend randomized. Since objective convex in log? no.

**Could use support vector machine style**: At current a, compute c and M. If active set A, we might reduce all active by optimizing a linear combination? The gradient of weighted active max as in LSE does that. Good.

**Potential local optimum conditions**: The best score maybe target 1.5027; local methods should reach. Let's think if our L-BFGS Fβ near max can make 0.5% improvement. Parent score 1.5109; target 1.5027 difference ~0.008. L-BFGS with 100 on 191 could. Need not guarantee. We can also use **randomized averaging of LP best responses** to escape.

**Softmax with pivot and z clipping**: In `_asoft_n1`, if z clipped to [-30,30], ratios limited e±30. If z from log ratios could exceed 30 if a_i/a_p >1e13; not likely if p max. But after updates, z could exceed; clip. Linear part denominator okay. If z too negative, a_i ~0; okay.

**Check gradient expression for pivot**: For variables i≠p, ∂a_i/∂z_j = a_i(δ_ij - a_j). ∂a_p/∂z_j = -a_p a_j. Gradient gz_j = g_i if i? sum: g_j a_j(1-a_j?) Wait compute: `a_j` variable (not pivot) derivative wrt z_j: `a_j(1 - a_j) + ∑_{i≠p,j}?` Let's derive for softmax with pivot p, `a_i = e^{z_i}/D`, `a_p=1/D`, D=1+∑e. ∂a_i/∂z_j = a_i δ_{ij}/D? Actually e_i/D; derivative: if j≠p, ∂D/∂z_j=e_j=a_j D. For i not p: ∂a_i = δ_ij e_j/D - e_i e_j/D^2 = a_j δ_ij - a_i a_j. For p: ∂a_p = -1 * ∂D /D^2 = -a_j. Thus total gz_j = a_j g_j - a_j ∑_{i≠p} a_i g_i - a_p g_p? Wait the second term from all i not p: -∑_{i≠p} a_i a_j g_i = -a_j ∑_{i≠p} a_i g_i. Plus p term -a_j g_p. Sum = a_j g_j - a_j (∑_{i≠p} a_i g_i + g_p) = a_j (g_j - ∑_{all i} a_i g_i). Yes `ga=dot(g,a)`, `gz=a_j*(g_j-ga)`. Good.

**F value**: If sum a exactly 1 but due to soft maybe. F uses c; M. F = M + log(s)/beta. This is LSE/β. If beta small, constant; okay.

**Minimize might evaluate at z causing overflow in exp(z) before clip**. We clip inside to [-30,30]; L-BFGS can propose huge z, but clip makes flat outside. Gradient zero? If z clipped flat, gradient of F with respect to z not correct beyond clip; but optimizer likely not extreme. Could use less clip [-50,50] and rely on exp safe? exp(50)=5e21, denominator sum maybe 1e24, still within float (1e308). exp(700) flows. Use clip [-50,50] safe. If optimum requires ratio >e50 (1e22) not needed.

def _calibrate(use_parent):
    try: eval_fn = evaluate_sequence
    except NameError: return False
    cands=[]
    cands.append(np.ones(N)/N)
    cands.append(np.ones(8)/8)

    d2=np.zeros(N); d2[0]=1; d2[-1]=1; cands.append(d2)

    ok=True

    return ok

**LP optimize code details**:
```
def _lp_optimize(a0, stop_time, max_iters=80, lp_timeout=3.0, rng=None):
    a=_normalize(a0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a); current_sc=best_sc
    n=a.size; m=2*n-1
    rows,cols,vals,m = _lp_indices(n)
    tol=1e-11
    stuck=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 2: break
       # compute c and M
       c=np.convolve(a,a); M=float(c.max())
       # fast score current (assume sum1)
       cs=2*n*M
       if cs < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=cs; stuck=0
       else:
          stuck += 1
       if stuck > 40: break
       if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0: break
       a_lp = a + 1e-12
       a_lp = a_lp/a_lp.sum()
       b_ub = np.full(m, M*(1+1e-9))
       data = a_lp[vals]
       try:
          A = csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(m,n))
          res = linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'time_limit': lp_timeout})
       except Exception:
          try:
             A=np.zeros((m,n)); A[rows,cols]=data
             res=linprog(...)
          except: res=None
       if res is None or res.x is None: break
       g=np.asarray(res.x,float); g=np.clip(g,0,None)
       gs=g.sum()
       if gs <= 1e-12: break
       gn=g/gs
       # maybe if LP returned g==a; no change
       if np.allclose(gn,a,atol=1e-7):
          # skip some?
          pass
       improved=False
       for t in (1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.25,0.15,0.1,0.05,0.02):
          cand=(1.0-t)*a + t*gn
          cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000); cs_=score(cand)
          if np.isfinite(cs_) and cs_ < cs - 1e-12:
             a=cand; cs=cs_; improved=True
             if cs < best_sc - 1e-12: best_sc=cs; best_a=a.copy(); stuck=0
             break
       if not improved:
          # try relaxed M?
          if it % 5 == 4:
             b_ub2 = np.full(m, M*(1+1e-5))
             ... solve once; if g diff, line search
          if stuck > 20:
             # perturb current a but keep best
             a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,n)); a=_normalize(a); stuck=0
             if score(a) > best_sc*1.05: a=best_a.copy()
    return best_a
```

**Pivot L-BFGS code**:
```
def _lbfgs_search(a0, score, stop_time, betas=(100,500,2000), maxiter=80, max_pivots=2):
    n=a0.size
    a=_normalize(a0); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
    # choose pivots
    pivots = [int(np.argmax(a)), n//2, 0, n-1]
    # remove duplicates
    unique=[]; for p in pivots: if p not unique and a[p]>1e-8: add; limit max_pivots
    rng = np.random.default_rng(...)
    if len(unique)<max_pivots: add random positive indices
    for p in unique:
       for beta in betas:
          if time>stop_time-15: break
          ap=np.maximum(a,1e-12); ap/=ap.sum()
          if ap[p] < 1e-10: continue
          data=np.arange(n); idx=data[data!=p]
          z0=np.log((ap[idx]/ap[p]) + 1e-300)
          z0 = np.clip(z0, -30, 30)
          def fun(z, p=p, idx=idx):
             if time.monotonic() > stop_time: raise _Abort
             e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-50,50)); D=e.sum()+1.0
             aa=np.empty(n); aa[idx]=e/D; aa[p]=1.0/D
             c=np.convolve(aa,aa); M=float(c.max())
             zc=beta*(c-M); zc=np.clip(zc,-700,0.0)
             ee=np.exp(zc); s=ee.sum()
             if s<=0 or not finite: ee[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; s=1.0
             w=ee/s
             F=M + np.log(s)/beta
             g=np.zeros(n)
             tol_w=1e-12
             # use active list maybe sorted
             act=np.nonzero(w > tol_w)[0]
             for k in act:
                 wk=w[k]
                 lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
                 g[lo:hi+1] += 2.0*wk*aa[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
             ga=float(np.dot(g,aa))
             gz=aa[idx]*(g[idx]-ga)
             # If gradient has nonfinite, set zeros
             if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)): gz=np.zeros_like(z)
             return F, gz
          try:
             res=minimize(fun, z0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':20, 'disp':False})
          except _Abort:
             res=None
          except Exception:
             res=None
          if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
             cand = _softmax_n1(...); sc=score(cand)
             if finite and sc < best_sc - 1e-12: best_a=cand; best_sc=sc; a=cand
          # If no improvement, maybe still use res.x if F lower but exact not? skip
    return best_a
```
Potential performance: In `fun`, active weights loop each time. For beta=100, with c differences maybe small, many active. Fine.
`ftol` objective F scale maybe ~6; fine.
`scope`: minimize may call fun at z0 before checking time; if stopTime close, okay.

def _softmax_n1(z, idx, p):
    e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-50,50)); D=e.sum()+1.0
    a=np.empty(len(idx)+1); a[idx]=e/D; a[p]=1/D; return a
```

def _direction_polish(a0, score, stop_time, max_iters=2000, betas=(200,1000)):
   a=norm; best...; n
   p=argmax; idx=...
   a_pos=max(a,1e-12); normalized; z=log ratios
   rng=default_rng
   no=0
   for beta in betas:
      for it in range(max_iters//len(betas)):
         if time>stop_time-5: break
         c=conv(a); cs=score(a) maybe directly; if cs<best update
         if no>100: # restart
            # perturb z
            z += rng.normal(0,0.1)
            a=softmax(z)
            if score(a)>best*1.02: a=best_a.copy(); z=log(best)
            no=0; continue
         w = softmax(beta*(c-c.max()))
         # Could choose top k active if beta high? no.
         g=grad_active(w,a)
         ga=dot(g,a)
         gz=a[idx]*(g[idx]-ga)
         mgn=max(abs(gz))
         if mgn>1e-12:
            base=0.1/mgn
            improved=False
            for mult in [1.0,0.5,2.0,0.25,4.0,0.125,8.0,0.0625]:
                alpha=mult*base
                if alpha>1e4: continue
                z_new=z - alpha*gz   # negative gradient
                cand=_softmax_n1(z_new,...)
                sc=score(cand)
                if finite and sc < cs - 1e-12: # or best?
                    # accept if improves current; update best if lower
                    a=cand; z=z_new; cs=sc; improved=True
                    if sc < best_sc - 1e-12: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
                    no=0
                    break
            if improved: continue
         # random move
         improved=False
         for scale in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.02]:
             d=rng.normal(size=n-1)
             cand=softmax(z + scale*d)
             sc=score(cand)
             if finite and sc < cs - 1e-12:
                 a=cand; z=...; cs=sc; improved=True; if sc<best update; no=0; break
         if not improved: no+=1
   return best_a
```

def _try_lengths(best_a, score, use_fast, stop_time, N):
   Ls = [N-1,N+1,N-2,N+2, max(8,int(0.75*N)), max(8,int(1.25*N))]
   for L in Ls:

Pseudo full code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix

class _Abort(Exception): pass
_LP_CACHE={}

def _norm(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0:
        return np.ones(1,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=0.0:
        return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
    return a/s

def _fast_score_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,..).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return inf
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    if not finite all: return inf
    s=a.sum()
    if s<0.01: return inf
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    mb=c.max()
    if not finite: return inf
    return 2.0*a.size*mb/(s*s)
```
Need `np.isfinite(a).all()` maybe if huge. Good.

`_parent`:
```
def _parent():
    try:
       h=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
       if h is not None:
           p=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
           p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
           p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
           if p.size>0 and p.sum()>0: return p
    except: pass

def _softmax_n1(z, idx, pivot):
    e=np.exp(np.clip(np.asarray(z,float), -50, 50))
    D=float(e.sum())+1.0
    a=np.empty(len(idx)+1,float)
    a[idx]=e/D
    a[pivot]=1.0/D
    return a

`_grad_weights(w,a)`:
```
def _grad_weights(w,a):
   n=a.size; g=np.zeros(n); m=w.size
   # If w sum not 1? normalize if >0
   ws=w.sum(); if ws>0: w=w/ws
   for k in range(m):
      wk=w[k]
      if wk <= 1e-13: continue
      lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
      g[lo:hi+1] += 2.0*wk*a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
   return g
```
Potential speed: `a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]` creates view reverse. Good.

```
def _lp_optimize(a0, score, stop_time, max_iters=80, lp_timeout=3.0, seed=0):
    n=a0.size
    a=_norm(a0); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a); rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    rows,cols,vals,m=_lp_indices(n)
    stuck=0; last_relax=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 2: break
       cs=score(a)
       if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=cs; stuck=0
       else: stuck += 1
       if stuck > 35: # perturb
          a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,n)); a=_norm(a)
          if score(a) > best_sc*1.05: a=best_a.copy(); stuck=0
          continue
       c=np.convolve(a,a); M=float(c.max())
       if not finite or M<=0: break
       a_lp=a+1e-12; a_lp/=a_lp.sum()
       b_ub=np.full(m, M*(1+1e-9))
       data=a_lp[vals]
       res=None
       try:
          A=csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(m,n))
          res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'time_limit': lp_timeout})
       except Exception:
          try:
             A=np.zeros((m,n)); A[rows,cols]=data
             res=linprog(... dense)
          except: res=None
       if res is None or res.x is None: break
       gn=np.clip(res.x,0,None); gs=gn.sum()
       if gs<=1e-12: break
       gn=gn/gs
       if np.allclose(gn,a,atol=1e-8):
          # try relaxed M
          if it % 7 == 6:
             b_ub2=np.full(m, M*(1+1e-5)); ... solve; if g2 improve; else stuck+=?
          else: continue
       improved=False
       for t in (1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.25,0.15,0.1,0.05,0.02):
          cand=(1-t)*a + t*gn
          cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000); cs2=score(cand)
          if np.isfinite(cs2) and cs2 < cs - 1e-12:
             a=cand; cs=cs2; improved=True
             if cs < best_sc -1e-12: best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=cs
             break
       if not improved:
          # maybe if cs2 lower than best but not current? unlikely; line search based current.
          if it % 5 == 4:
             # relaxed
             ... solve with b_ub*(1+1e-4)
             if success and improves, accept
             stuck += 1
    return best_a, best_sc? We need update best in propose. Return best_a.
```

def _solve_lp(a, M, timeout, n=None):
   if n is None: n=a.size
   rows,cols,vals,m=_lp_indices(n)
   b_ub=np.full(m,M)
   cvec=-np.ones(n)
   bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n
   opts={'time_limit': float(timeout)}
   data=a[vals]
   try:
      A=csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(m,n))
      res=linprog(cvec,A_ub=A,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options=opts)
   except Exception:
      A=np.zeros((m,n)); A[rows,cols]=data
      res=linprog(...)
   if res is None or res.x is None: return None
   x=np.asarray(res.x); x=np.clip(x,0,None)
   s=x.sum()
   if s<=1e-12: return None
   return x/s
```

def _make_inits(parent, N, seed):
   inits=[]
   p=_norm(parent if parent.size==N else _resample(parent,N))
   inits.append(p.copy())
   inits.append(np.ones(N)/N)
   x=np.arange(N); x=x/max(1,N-1)
   for amp in [0.2,0.5,0.8]:
       w=1.0 + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
       w=np.maximum(w,1e-3); inits.append(p*0? no, w/w.sum())
   # U-shaped
   u=0.2 + 1.8*np.abs(x-0.5)**2
   inits.append(u/u.sum())
   # inverse U? center high
   v=0.2 + 1.8*np.exp(-20*(x-0.5)**2)
   inits.append(v/v.sum())
   # blend parent with U
   for lam in [0.7,0.4]:
       b=lam*p + (1-lam)*u; inits.append(b/b.sum())
   # noise parent
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   for sig in [0.1,0.3]:
       b=p*np.exp(rng.normal(0,sig,N)); b=np.maximum(b,1e-4); inits.append(b/b.sum())
   # random smooth: low-pass random
   r=rng.normal(0,1,N)
   kernel=np.array([0.1,0.35,0.1])? Use conv reflect.
   # Simple exponential smoothing: for passes, b=(r+r_smooth)?
   b=np.convolve(np.maximum(r,0), np.array([0.2,0.6,0.2]), mode='same'); b=np.maximum(b,1e-3); inits.append(b/b.sum())
   # QR-ish if N prime
   if _is_prime(N) and N%2==1:
       qr=np.zeros(N); a=1; for i in range(1,N): # compute squares
       for q in squares: qr[q]=1
       w=0.5 + qr; w[0]=1? normalize
       inits.append(w/w.sum())
   return inits
```

Let's implement `_conv_self(a)` using FFT if n > 256 perhaps, else direct. This adds robustness.
```
def _conv_self(a):
   n=a.size
   if n <= 256:
       return np.convolve(a,a)
   f=max(1,int(2**np.ceil(np.log2(2*n-1)))) # or next_fast_len
   F=np.fft.rfft(a,f)
   c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,f)[:2*n-1]
   np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
   return c
```
But direct np.convolve for 191 probably faster than FFT overhead. Use direct for n<512. Simpler: always direct to avoid numerical negative. n=191. Keep direct.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0: budget = 10.0
    reserve = max(5.0, 0.05*budget)
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget - reserve)
    parent = _get_parent()
    N0 = parent.size
    if N0 < 4 or N0 > 512:
       if N0 >=4:
          tmp = np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,191), np.linspace(0,1,N0), parent)
       else: tmp=np.ones(191)
       N=191; parent = tmp
    else:
       N=N0; parent=parent
    p0=_norm(parent)
    # if p0 invalid uniform
    # calibration
    use_fast = _calibrate(N, p0)
    score = _make_score(use_fast)
    best_a = p0.copy()
    best_sc = score(best_a)
    # probes
    probes = [[1.0], np.ones(N)/N, p0.copy()]
    inits = _make_inits(p0,N,seed)
    cands = inits + probes
    scored=[]
    for a in cands:
       an=_norm(a)
       if an.size == 0: continue
       sc=score(an)
       if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,an.copy()))
    scored.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
    for sc,a in scored:
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
    # helper consider
    def consider(a, sc=None):
       nonlocal best_a,best_sc
       an=_norm(a)
       if an.size==0: return
       if sc is None: sc=score(an)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
           best_sc=float(sc); best_a=an.copy()
    # pool
    pool = [a for sc,a in scored if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_sc*1.02 + 1e-9]
    if len(pool)>12: pool = pool[:12]
    def add_pool(a, sc=None):
       nonlocal maybe
       an=_norm(a)
       if sc is None: sc=score(an)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_sc*1.03 + 1e-9 and len(pool)<20: pool.append(an.copy())
```
Nested functions with nonlocal okay in Python 3.
But top-level helpers cannot use nested `consider`; call from propose.

```
def _split_endpoints(start, end, k):
    if k<=1: return [end]
    return [start + (end-start)*(i+1)/k for i in range(k)]
```

**Let's code `_is_prime` simple**:
```
def _is_prime(p):
   if p<2: return False
   if p%2==0: return p==2
   i=3
   while i*i<=p:
      if p%i==0: return False
      i+=2
   return True
```
For N=191 fine.

def _blend_pool(pool, score, consider, stop_time):
    m=min(len(pool),12)
    for i in range(m):
       for j in range(i+1,m):
          if time.monotonic()>stop_time-2: return
          ai=pool[i]; aj=pool[j]

def _direction_polish(a0, score, use_fast, stop_time, max_iters=2000, betas=(200,1000), seed=0):
    a=_norm(a0); n=a.size
    if n<=1: return a
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a); cs=best_sc
    p=int(np.argmax(a)); idx=np.array([i for i in range(n) if i!=p], dtype=int)
    ap=np.maximum(a,1e-12); ap/=ap.sum()
    if ap[p] <= 1e-12: ap = np.ones(n)/n; p=n//2; idx=...
    z=np.log(ap[idx]/ap[p]); z=np.clip(z,-30,30)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no=0
    max_per_beta=max(50, max_iters//len(betas))
    for beta in betas:
       for it in range(max_per_beta):
          if time.monotonic()>stop_time-3: break
          c=np.convolve(a,a)
          if use_fast:
             s=a.sum(); cs = 2*n*float(c.max())/(s*s) if s>1e-12 else inf
          else:
             cs=score(a)
          if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best_sc=cs; best_a=a.copy(); no=0
          else: no +=1
          if no > 80:
             # perturb
             z = z + rng.normal(0,0.1,size=n-1)
             a = _softmax_n1(z, idx, p)
             if not np.isfinite(score(a))? score maybe; if score(a) > best_sc*1.05: a=best_a.copy(); z=np.log(best_a[idx]/best_a[p])
             no=0
             continue
          M=float(c.max())
          if not finite or M<=0: break
          zc=beta*(c-M); zc=np.clip(zc,-700,0); ee=np.exp(zc); ssum=ee.sum(); if bad: ee[argmax]=1; ssum=1
          w=ee/ssum
          g=_grad_weights(w,a)
          ga=float(np.dot(g,a))
          gz=a[idx]*(g[idx]-ga)
          mgn=float(np.max(np.abs(gz)))
          acted=False
          if mgn > 1e-12:
             base=0.1/mgn
             for mult in (1.0,0.5,2.0,0.25,4.0,0.125,8.0):
                znew=z - (mult*base)*gz
                cand=_softmax_n1(znew, idx, p)
                sc=score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cs - 1e-12:
                   a=cand; z=znew; cs=sc; acted=True
                   if sc < best_sc - 1e-12: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
                   no=0
                   break
             if acted: continue
          # random
          acted=False
          for scale in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.02):
             dd=rng.normal(size=n-1)
             cand=_softmax_n1(z + scale*dd, idx, p)
             sc=score(cand)
             if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cs - 1e-12:
                a=cand; z=z+scale*dd; cs=sc; acted=True
                if sc < best_sc -1e-12: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
                no=0
                break
          if not acted:
             no += 1
             if no > 80:
                z = z + rng.normal(0,0.2,size=n-1); a=_softmax_n1(...)
    return best_a
```
Need update z when a=best_a fallback: `z=np.log(best_a[idx]/best_a[p])` but best_a[p] may small; use eps. Implement helper `_z_from_a(a, idx, p)`:
```
def _z_from_a(a, idx, p):
   ap=np.maximum(a,1e-12); ap/=ap.sum()
   denom=np.maximum(ap[p],1e-300)
   z=np.log(np.maximum(ap[idx]/denom,1e-300))
   return np.clip(z,-30,30)
```

def _lp_indices(n):
   if n in _LP_CACHE: return _LP_CACHE[n]
   m=2*n-1
   rows=[]; cols=[]; vals=[]
   for k in range(m):
      lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
      for i in range(lo,hi+1):
         rows.append(k); cols.append(i); vals.append(k-i)
   rows=np.asarray(rows,dtype=np.int64); ...
   _LP_CACHE[n]=(rows,cols,vals,m)
   return ...
```

`_solve_lp(a, M, timeout)`:
```
def _solve_lp(a, M, timeout=2.0):
   n=a.size
   if n<=0 or M<=0 or not finite: return None
   rows,cols,vals,m=_lp_indices(n)
   cvec=-np.ones(n)
   b_ub=np.full(m, float(M), dtype=np.float64)
   bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n
   opts={'time_limit': float(timeout), 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8}
   data=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)[vals]
   res=None
   try:
      A=csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(m,n))
      res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=opts)
   except Exception:
      try:
         A=np.zeros((m,n), dtype=np.float64)
         A[rows,cols]=data
         res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=opts)
      except Exception:
         res=None
   if res is None or getattr(res,'x',None) is None: return None
   x=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return None
   x=np.clip(x,0.0,None)
   s=float(x.sum())
   if s<=1e-12: return None
   return x/s
```
Some HiGHS options may warn if unknown? `dual_feasibility_tolerance` accepted? I think in scipy linprog highs options: `time_limit`, `dual_feasibility_tolerance`, `primal_feasibility_tolerance`, `psd_rho`? Should. If error, catch? But options passed to linprog; if invalid, linprog raises ValueError, caught and dense fallback with same invalid options also raises. Maybe better remove optional tolerance to be safe, just time_limit. HiGHS options in scipy: `time_limit`, `dual_feasibility_tolerance`, `primal_feasibility_tolerance`, `mip_rel_gap`, etc. Should be okay. But to avoid version issues, use `options={'time_limit': float(timeout)}` only. Simpler. Trust default tolerances. Let's use just time_limit.

`_make_inits`: Use parent normalized. For QR, p int. Need `np.arange(1, p)`; for p=1 no. Weight arrays.
```
def _make_inits(parent, n, seed):
    p0=_norm(parent)
    inits=[p0.copy(), np.ones(n)/n]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n) if n>1 else np.array([0.0])
    for amp in (0.2,0.5,0.8):
        w=1+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*x); w=np.maximum(w,1e-4); inits.append(w/w.sum())
    u=0.2+1.8*(2*x-1)**2; inits.append(u/u.sum())
    v=0.2+1.8*np.exp(-30*(x-0.5)**2); inits.append(v/v.sum())
    for lam in (0.7,0.4):
       b=lam*p0+(1-lam)*(u/u.sum()); inits.append(_norm(b))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    for sig in (0.1,0.3):
       b=p0*np.exp(rng.normal(0,sig,n)); b=np.maximum(b,1e-8); inits.append(_norm(b))
    r=rng.normal(0,1,n)
    ker=np.array([0.2,0.6,0.2]); b=np.convolve(np.maximum(r,0.0), ker, mode='same'); b=np.maximum(b,1e-4); inits.append(_norm(b))
    if _is_prime(n) and n>=3:
       qr=np.zeros(n); for q in range(1,n): qr[(q*q)%n]=1.0
       w=0.3+0.7*qr; inits.append(_norm(w))
       w2=0.3+0.7*(1.0-qr); inits.append(_norm(w2))
    return inits
```
Mode='same' output length n; for n=1, okay? np.convolve with mode='same' length1. `x` if n=1. `_is_prime(1)` false.

**`_resample`**:
```
def _resample(a,L):
    a=_norm(a)
    if a.size==L: return a.copy()
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(L)/L
    x0=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); x1=np.linspace(0,1,L)
    b=np.interp(x1,x0,a)
    return _norm(b)
```

**Let's implement `_fd_polish` carefully**:
```
def _fd_polish(a0, score, stop_time, max_iters=60, h=1e-3, seed=0):
   a=_norm(a0); n=a.size
   if n<3: return a
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a); cs=best_sc
   p=int(np.argmax(a)); idx=np.array([...])
   z=_z_from_a(a,idx,p)
   rng=...
   no=0
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time>stop-3: break
      cs=score(a); if cs<best: update; else no++
      if no > 50: perturb; no=0; continue
      grad=np.zeros(n-1)
      for i in range(n-1):
         if time>stop-2: break
         zp=z.copy(); zm=z.copy(); zp[i]+=h; zm[i]-=h
         fp=score(_softmax_n1(zp,idx,p)); fm=score(_softmax_n1(zm,idx,p))
         if np.isfinite(fp) and np.isfinite(fm): grad[i]=(fp-fm)/(2*h)
         elif np.isfinite(fp): grad[i]=(fp-cs)/h
         elif np.isfinite(fm): grad[i]=(cs-fm)/h
      mgn=max abs grad
      if mgn < 1e-12: break
      alpha=0.1/mgn
      improved=False
      for mult in (1,0.5,2,0.25,4):
         znew=z - mult*alpha*grad
         cand=_softmax_n1(znew,idx,p)
         sc=score(cand)
         if finite and sc < cs -1e-11: accept; improve; break
      if not: random similar
   return best_a
```
If `cs` current inf? break. If exact evaluation for base score inf, return best. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix


class _Abort(Exception):
    pass


_LP_CACHE = {}


def _norm(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _get_parent():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size > 0 and float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
                return p
    except Exception:
        pass
    return np.ones(191, dtype=np.float64)


def _is_prime(p):
    p = int(p)
    if p < 2:
        return False
    if p % 2 == 0:
        return p == 2
    i = 3
    while i * i <= p:
        if p % i == 0:
            return False
        i += 2
    return True


def _resample(a, L):
    a = _norm(a)
    L = int(max(1, L))
    if a.size == L:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L)
    b = np.interp(x1, x0, a)
    return _norm(b)


def _fast_score_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    mb = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * mb / (s * s))


def _exact_score_list(lst):
    try:
        fn = globals().get("evaluate_sequence")
        if fn is None or not callable(fn):
            return float("inf")
        return float(fn(lst))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _make_score(use_fast):
    if use_fast:
        def sc(a):
            a = _norm(a)
            if a.size == 0:
                return float("inf")
            return _fast_score_array(a)
        return sc

    def sc(a):
        a = _norm(a)
        if a.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        return _exact_score_list([float(x) for x in a])

    return sc


def _calibrate(n, p0):
    try:
        fn = globals().get("evaluate_sequence")
    except Exception:
        return False
    if fn is None or not callable(fn):
        return False

    cands = [
        np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n),
        np.ones(8, dtype=np.float64) / 8.0,
    ]
    d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    d[0] = 1.0
    cands.append(d)
    d2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    d2[0] = 1.0
    d2[-1] = 1.0
    if n > 1:
        cands.append(d2 / 2.0)
    if p0 is not None and p0.size == n:
        cands.append(np.clip(p0, 0.0, 1000.0) * 3.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    r = rng.random(n) + 0.1
    cands.append(r)
    cands.append(np.clip(r * 1500.0, 0.0, 1000.0))

    ok = True
    seen = 0
    for arr in cands:
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0:
            continue
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s < 1e-9:
            arr = np.ones_like(arr)
        lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
        sf = _fast_score_array(lst)
        se = _exact_score_list(lst)
        if not (np.isfinite(sf) and np.isfinite(se)):
            continue
        seen += 1
        if abs(sf - se) > 1e-6 * max(1.0, abs(sf)):
            ok = False
    return bool(ok and seen >= 2)


def _lp_indices(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n in _LP_CACHE:
        return _LP_CACHE[n]
    m = 2 * n - 1
    rows = []
    cols = []
    vals = []
    for k in range(m):
        lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        for i in range(lo, hi + 1):
            rows.append(k)
            cols.append(i)
            vals.append(k - i)
    rows = np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64)
    cols = np.asarray(cols, dtype=np.int64)
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.int64)
    _LP_CACHE[n] = (rows, cols, vals, m)
    return _LP_CACHE[n]


def _solve_lp(a, M, timeout=2.0):
    a = _norm(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0 or not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None
    rows, cols, vals, m = _lp_indices(n)
    cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.full(m, float(M), dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    opts = {"time_limit": float(timeout)}
    data = a[vals]
    res = None

    try:
        A = csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(m, n))
        res = linprog(
            cvec,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        res = None

    if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        try:
            A = np.zeros((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
            A[rows, cols] = data
            res = linprog(
                cvec,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options=opts,
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

    if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None
    x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return None
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return x / s


def _softmax_n1(z, idx, pivot):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0))
    denom = float(e.sum()) + 1.0
    a = np.empty(int(idx.size) + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    a[idx] = e / denom
    a[pivot] = 1.0 / denom
    return a


def _z_from_a(a, idx, pivot):
    a = _norm(a)
    ap = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    ap = ap / float(ap.sum())
    denom = max(float(ap[pivot]), 1e-300)
    ratios = np.maximum(ap[idx] / denom, 1e-300)
    z = np.log(ratios)
    return np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)


def _grad_weights(w, a):
    n = a.size
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size != 2 * n - 1:
        w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
        w = np.zeros_like(w)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 0.0:
        k0 = int(np.argmax(a * a)) if n > 0 else 0
        if k0 < 0 or k0 >= w.size:
            k0 = 0
        w[k0] = 1.0
        ws = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / ws

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(w.size):
        wk = float(w[k])
        if wk <= 1e-13:
            continue
        lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi < lo:
            continue
        seg = a[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]
        g[lo:hi + 1] += 2.0 * wk * seg
    return g


def _make_inits(parent, n, seed):
    p0 = _norm(parent)
    inits = [p0.copy(), np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]
    if n > 1:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    else:
        x = np.array([0.0])

    for amp in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8):
        w = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
        w = np.maximum(w, 1e-4)
        inits.append(w / float(w.sum()))

    u = 0.2 + 1.8 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2
    inits.append(u / float(u.sum()))

    v = 0.2 + 1.8 * np.exp(-30.0 * (x - 0.5) ** 2)
    inits.append(v / float(v.sum()))

    for lam in (0.7, 0.4):
        b = lam * p0 + (1.0 - lam) * (u / float(u.sum()))
        inits.append(_norm(b))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    for sig in (0.1, 0.3):
        b = p0 * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, n))
        b = np.maximum(b, 1e-8)
        inits.append(_norm(b))

    r = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    ker = np.array([0.2, 0.6, 0.2], dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.convolve(np.maximum(r, 0.0), ker, mode="same")
    b = np.maximum(b, 1e-4)
    inits.append(_norm(b))

    if _is_prime(n) and n >= 3:
        qr = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for q in range(1, n):
            qr[(q * q) % n] = 1.0
        w = 0.3 + 0.7 * qr
        inits.append(_norm(w))
        w = 0.3 + 0.7 * (1.0 - qr)
        inits.append(_norm(w))

    return inits


def _lp_optimize(a0, score, stop_time, max_iters=100, lp_timeout=3.0, seed=0):
    a = _norm(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_sc = score(best_a)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    stuck = 0
    fail = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 2.0:
            break

        cs = score(a)
        if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = float(cs)
            best_a = a.copy()
            stuck = 0
        else:
            stuck += 1

        if stuck > 60:
            break

        if stuck > 35:
            a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, n))
            a = _norm(a)
            sau = score(a)
            if not np.isfinite(sau) or sau > best_sc * 1.05:
                a = best_a.copy()
            stuck = 0
            continue

        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        a_lp = a + 1e-12
        a_lp = a_lp / float(a_lp.sum())

        gn = None
        for mult in (1.0 + 1e-9, 1.0 + 1e-6, 1.0 + 1e-4):
            gn = _solve_lp(a_lp, M * mult, lp_timeout)
            if gn is not None:
                break

        if gn is None:
            fail += 1
            if fail > 8:
                break
            continue

        if np.allclose(gn, a, rtol=1e-6, atol=1e-7):
            fail += 1
            if fail > 12:
                break
            continue

        ok_step = False
        for t in (1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.15, 0.10, 0.05, 0.02):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * gn
            cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
            cand = _norm(cand)
            val = score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(val) and val < cs - 1e-12:
                a = cand
                cs = float(val)
                if cs < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = cs
                    best_a = a.copy()
                ok_step = True
                break

        if not ok_step:
            fail += 1
            if fail > 20:
                break

    return best_a


def _lbfgs_search(a0, score, stop_time, betas=(100.0, 500.0, 2000.0),
                  maxiter=70, max_pivots=2, seed=0):
    a = _norm(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 2:
        return a

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score(a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    cands = []
    for p in (int(np.argmax(a)), n // 2, 0, n - 1, int(rng.integers(0, n))):
        p = int(max(0, min(n - 1, p)))
        if p not in cands:
            cands.append(p)
    pivots = cands[:max(1, int(max_pivots))]

    for p in pivots:
        for beta in betas:
            if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 15.0:
                break
            idx = np.array([i for i in range(n) if i != p], dtype=int)
            z0 = _z_from_a(a, idx, p)
            before = best_sc

            def fun(z, idx=idx, p=p, beta=float(beta)):
                if time.monotonic() > stop_time:
                    raise _Abort()
                z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                if z.size != idx.size or not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(z.size, dtype=np.float64)

                aa = _softmax_n1(z, idx, p)
                c = np.convolve(aa, aa)
                M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
                if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(z.size, dtype=np.float64)

                zc = beta * (c - M)
                zc = np.clip(zc, -700.0, 0.0)
                ee = np.exp(zc)
                ssum = float(ee.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0.0:
                    ee = np.zeros_like(ee)
                    ee[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
                    ssum = 1.0
                w = ee / ssum
                F = M + float(np.log(ssum)) / beta

                g = _grad_weights(w, aa)
                ga = float(np.dot(g, aa))
                gz = aa[idx] * (g[idx] - ga)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
                    gz = np.zeros(z.size, dtype=np.float64)
                return F, gz

            res = None
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    fun,
                    z0,
                    jac=True,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    options={
                        "maxiter": int(max(maxiter, 1)),
                        "ftol": 1e-12,
                        "gtol": 1e-8,
                        "maxls": 20,
                        "disp": False,
                    },
                )
            except _Abort:
                res = None
            except Exception:
                res = None

            if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                if x.size == idx.size and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                    cand = _softmax_n1(x, idx, p)
                    val = score(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_sc + 1e-7:
                        a = cand
                        if val < best_sc - 1e-12:
                            best_sc = float(val)
                            best_a = cand.copy()

            if best_sc >= before - 1e-13:
                pert = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n))
                pert = _norm(pert)
                pv = score(pert)
                if np.isfinite(pv) and pv <= best_sc * 1.02:
                    a = pert
                else:
                    a = best_a.copy()

            if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 15.0:
                break

    return best_a


def _direction_polish(a0, score, use_fast, stop_time, max_iters=2000,
                      betas=(200.0, 1000.0, 3000.0), seed=0):
    a = _norm(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score(a)
    cs = best_sc
    p = int(np.argmax(a))
    idx = np.array([i for i in range(n) if i != p], dtype=int)
    z = _z_from_a(a, idx, p)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve = 0
    per = max(50, int(max_iters) // max(1, len(betas)))

    for beta in betas:
        for _ in range(per):
            if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 3.0:
                break

            c = np.convolve(a, a)
            if use_fast:
                s = float(a.sum())
                cs = float(2.0 * n * c.max() / (s * s)) if (s > 1e-12 and c.size) else float("inf")
            else:
                cs = score(a)

            if not np.isfinite(cs):
                break
            if cs < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = float(cs)
                best_a = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1

            if no_improve > 80:
                z = z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, idx.size)
                a = _softmax_n1(z, idx, p)
                sau = score(a)
                if not np.isfinite(sau) or sau > best_sc * 1.05:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    z = _z_from_a(a, idx, p)
                no_improve = 0
                continue

            M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
                break

            zc = beta * (c - M)
            zc = np.clip(zc, -700.0, 0.0)
            ee = np.exp(zc)
            ssum = float(ee.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0.0:
                ee = np.zeros_like(ee)
                ee[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
                ssum = 1.0
            w = ee / ssum

            g = _grad_weights(w, a)
            ga = float(np.dot(g, a))
            gz = a[idx] * (g[idx] - ga)
            mgn = float(np.max(np.abs(gz))) if gz.size else 0.0
            acted = False

            if mgn > 1e-12:
                base = 0.1 / mgn
                for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25, 4.0, 0.125, 8.0):
                    znew = z - (mult * base) * gz
                    cand = _softmax_n1(znew, idx, p)
                    val = score(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(val) and val < cs - 1e-12:
                        a = cand
                        z = znew
                        cs = float(val)
                        acted = True
                        if cs < best_sc - 1e-12:
                            best_sc = cs
                            best_a = a.copy()
                        no_improve = 0
                        break
                if acted:
                    continue

            for scale in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.02):
                d = rng.normal(size=idx.size)
                znew = z + scale * d
                cand = _softmax_n1(znew, idx, p)
                val = score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(val) and val < cs - 1e-12:
                    a = cand
                    z = znew
                    cs = float(val)
                    acted = True
                    if cs < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best_sc = cs
                        best_a = a.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                    break

            if not acted:
                no_improve += 1

        if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 3.0:
            break

    return best_a


def _fd_polish(a0, score, stop_time, max_iters=60, h=1e-3, seed=0):
    a = _norm(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 2:
        return a
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_sc = score(best_a)

    p = int(np.argmax(best_a))
    idx = np.array([i for i in range(n) if i != p], dtype=int)
    z = _z_from_a(best_a, idx, p)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 3.0:
            break

        cs = score(a)
        if not np.isfinite(cs):
            a = best_a.copy()
            z = _z_from_a(a, idx, p)
            cs = best_sc
            no_improve += 1
        else:
            if cs < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = float(cs)
                best_a = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 50:
            z = z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.15, idx.size)
            a = _softmax_n1(z, idx, p)
            au = score(a)
            if not np.isfinite(au) or au > best_sc * 1.05:
                a = best_a.copy()
                z = _z_from_a(a, idx, p)
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        grad = np.zeros(idx.size, dtype=np.float64)
        for ii in range(idx.size):
            if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 2.0:
                break
            zp = z.copy()
            zm = z.copy()
            zp[ii] += h
            zm[ii] -= h
            fp = score(_softmax_n1(zp, idx, p))
            fm = score(_softmax_n1(zm, idx, p))
            if np.isfinite(fp) and np.isfinite(fm):
                grad[ii] = (fp - fm) / (2.0 * h)
            elif np.isfinite(fp):
                grad[ii] = (fp - cs) / h
            elif np.isfinite(fm):
                grad[ii] = (cs - fm) / h

        mgn = float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(mgn) or mgn <= 1e-12:
            break

        alpha = 0.1 / mgn
        ok_step = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25, 4.0):
            znew = z - (mult * alpha) * grad
            cand = _softmax_n1(znew, idx, p)
            val = score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(val) and val < cs - 1e-11:
                a = cand
                z = znew
                cs = float(val)
                if val < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = val
                    best_a = a.copy()
                ok_step = True
                break

        if not ok_step:
            for scale in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2):
                d = rng.normal(size=idx.size)
                znew = z + scale * d
                cand = _softmax_n1(znew, idx, p)
                val = score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(val) and val < cs - 1e-11:
                    a = cand
                    z = znew
                    cs = float(val)
                    if val < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best_sc = val
                        best_a = a.copy()
                    ok_step = True
                    break

        if not ok_step:
            no_improve += 1

    return best_a


def _blend_pool(pool, score, consider, stop_time):
    if not pool:
        return
    m = min(len(pool), 12)
    for i in range(m):
        for j in range(i + 1, m):
            if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 2.0:
                return
            ai = pool[i]
            aj = pool[j]
            if ai.size != aj.size:
                if ai.size > aj.size:
                    aj = _resample(aj, ai.size)
                else:
                    ai = _resample(ai, aj.size)
            for t in (0.5, 0.3, 0.7):
                if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 2.0:
                    break
                consider((1.0 - t) * ai + t * aj)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 1300.0

    reserve = min(0.10 * budget, max(5.0, 0.05 * budget))
    deadline = t0 + max(0.1, budget - reserve)

    seed = int(seed) % (2 ** 32)

    raw_parent = _get_parent()
    if 4 <= raw_parent.size <= 512:
        N = int(raw_parent.size)
        parent = raw_parent
    else:
        N = 191
        if raw_parent.size >= 4:
            parent = np.interp(
                np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N),
                np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, raw_parent.size),
                raw_parent,
            )
        else:
            parent = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)

    p0 = _norm(parent)
    use_fast = _calibrate(N, p0)
    score = _make_score(use_fast)

    best_a = p0.copy()
    best_sc = score(best_a)

    def consider(a, sc=None):
        nonlocal best_a, best_sc
        an = _norm(a)
        if an.size == 0:
            return
        if sc is None:
            sc = score(an)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = an.copy()

    pool = []

    def add_pool(a, sc=None):
        an = _norm(a)
        if an.size == 0:
            return
        if sc is None:
            sc = score(an)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_sc * 1.03 + 1e-9 and len(pool) < 24:
            pool.append(an.copy())

    inits = _make_inits(p0, N, seed)
    probes = [
        np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64),
        np.array([1.0, 0.0], dtype=np.float64),
        np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N),
        p0.copy(),
    ]

    cands = inits + probes
    scored = []
    seen = set()
    for a in cands:
        an = _norm(a)
        if an.size == 0 or an.size > 512:
            continue
        key = an.tobytes()
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        sc = score(an)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), an.copy()))

    scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    for sc, an in scored:
        consider(an, sc)
        add_pool(an, sc)

    avail = max(0.0, deadline - t0)
    now = time.monotonic()

    run_starts = []
    for a in [p0] + [an for _, an in scored]:
        if a.size != N or a.size < 3:
            continue
        if all(np.linalg.norm(a - b) > 1e-8 for b in run_starts):
            run_starts.append(a)
        if len(run_starts) >= 2:
            break

    t_lp = t0 + 0.34 * avail
    if len(run_starts) > 0 and now < t_lp - 8.0:
        rem = len(run_starts)
        for a in run_starts:
            if now > t_lp - 6.0:
                break
            end = min(t_lp, now + (t_lp - now) / float(rem) + 2.0)
            if end - now > 8.0:
                cand = _lp_optimize(
                    a,
                    score,
                    end,
                    max_iters=40 if N > 300 else 120,
                    lp_timeout=2.0 if N > 300 else 3.0,
                    seed=seed + len(run_starts),
                )
                consider(cand)
                add_pool(cand)
            rem -= 1
            now = time.monotonic()

    starts = [best_a]
    for sc, a in scored:
        if a.size != best_a.size or a.size < 3:
            continue
        if np.linalg.norm(a - starts[0]) > 1e-8:
            starts.append(a)
        if len(starts) >= 2:
            break

    t_qn = t0 + 0.64 * avail
    if starts and now < t_qn - 15.0:
        rem = len(starts)
        for a in starts:
            if now > t_qn - 12.0:
                break
            end = min(t_qn, now + (t_qn - now) / float(rem) + 2.0)
            if end - now > 12.0:
                if use_fast:
                    cand = _lbfgs_search(
                        a,
                        score,
                        end,
                        betas=(200.0, 1000.0) if N > 300 else (100.0, 500.0, 2000.0),
                        maxiter=30 if N > 300 else 70,
                        max_pivots=1 if N > 300 else 2,
                        seed=seed + 1000 + len(starts),
                    )
                else:
                    cand = _fd_polish(a, score, end, max_iters=40 if N > 300 else 60, seed=seed + 2000)
                consider(cand)
                add_pool(cand)
            rem -= 1
            now = time.monotonic()

    t_dir = t0 + 0.84 * avail
    if now < t_dir - 8.0:
        end_dir = min(t_dir, now + max(20.0, 0.25 * avail))
        if use_fast:
            cand = _direction_polish(
                best_a,
                score,
                True,
                end_dir,
                max_iters=800 if N > 300 else 2500,
                betas=(500.0, 2000.0) if N > 300 else (200.0, 1000.0, 3000.0),
                seed=seed + 3000,
            )
        else:
            cand = _fd_polish(best_a, score, end_dir, max_iters=40, seed=seed + 3000)
        consider(cand)
        add_pool(cand)

    t_blend = t0 + 0.91 * avail
    if pool and now < t_blend - 3.0:
        _blend_pool(pool, score, consider, min(t_blend, now + max(8.0, 0.05 * avail)))

    if now < deadline - 15.0 and best_a.size >= 3:
        end_lp2 = min(deadline - 10.0, now + max(8.0, 0.03 * avail))
        if end_lp2 - now > 8.0:
            cand = _lp_optimize(
                best_a,
                score,
                end_lp2,
                max_iters=12 if N > 300 else 25,
                lp_timeout=2.0,
                seed=seed + 99999,
            )
            consider(cand)
            add_pool(cand)

    if now < deadline - 25.0:
        B = int(best_a.size)
        for L in (B - 1, B + 1, B - 2, B + 2, N):
            if now > deadline - 12.0:
                break
            if L < 1 or L > 512:
                continue
            init = _resample(best_a, L)
            consider(init)
            add_pool(init)
            if use_fast and now < deadline - 25.0:
                end_len = min(deadline - 12.0, now + 12.0)
                if end_len - now > 6.0:
                    cand = _direction_polish(
                        init,
                        score,
                        True,
                        end_len,
                        max_iters=250,
                        betas=(1000.0,),
                        seed=seed + L,
                    )
                    consider(cand)
                    add_pool(cand)

    final_candidates = list(pool)
    final_candidates += [
        best_a,
        p0,
        _norm(parent),
        np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N),
        np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64),
    ]

    exact_best_a = None
    exact_best_sc = float("inf")
    seen_final = set()
    for a in final_candidates:
        an = _norm(a)
        if an.size == 0 or an.size > 512:
            continue
        key = an.tobytes()
        if key in seen_final:
            continue
        seen_final.add(key)
        sc = _exact_score_list([float(x) for x in an])
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < exact_best_sc - 1e-15:
            exact_best_sc = float(sc)
            exact_best_a = an.copy()

    if exact_best_a is None:
        exact_best_a = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
        exact_best_sc = _exact_score_list([float(x) for x in exact_best_a])

    Lbest = int(exact_best_a.size)
    u = np.ones(Lbest, dtype=np.float64) / float(Lbest)
    u_sc = _exact_score_list([float(x) for x in u])

    if not np.isfinite(exact_best_sc) or (np.isfinite(u_sc) and exact_best_sc > u_sc + 1e-12):
        exact_best_a = u
        exact_best_sc = u_sc

    final_a = _norm(exact_best_a)
    if final_a.size == 0:
        final_a = np.ones(191, dtype=np.float64) / 191.0

    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_a]
    try:
        fs = _exact_score_list(final_list)
    except Exception:
        fs = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(fs) or len(final_list) == 0:
        final_list = [1.0 / float(N) for _ in range(N)]

    return final_list
```
